# agent_2: the whole run

[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer is \mathcal D=\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}: n\ge3,\ c\ge6\}. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it takes two halves: for every \(n\ge3\) and \(c\ge6\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\), and for every \(n\ge2\) and \(c\ge1\) with \(n=2\) or \(c\le5\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)=\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\). Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

```
#14 agent_4:
Rank2 proof complete modulo classical Nielsen/Magnus–Witt facts. Magnus–Witt identifies grγ(F_n) with free Lie subring L⊂Z⟨X1,…,Xn⟩. Its positive-degree center is zero: a homogeneous associative polynomial commuting with X1 must be a multiple of X1^d (rotate leading X1's in each word's coefficient until reaching a word starting with another letter, which must have coefficient zero); commuting with X2 then forces zero. Thus if [g,F_n]⊂γ_{c+2} but g∉γ_{c+1}, take r≤c with g∈γr\γr+1; its nonzero degree-r leading class commutes with all Xi, contradiction. Hence Z(F_n/γ_{c+2})=γ_{c+1}/γ_{c+2}. Since IA2=InnF2≅F2 and γc Inn=Inn γc, γc(IA2)∩A_{c+1}=Inn γ_{c+1}=γ_{c+1}(IA2), every c. Exact free-word certificates are workspace/rank2_certificate.py.
```

```
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/degree1_certificate.py')
p.write_text('''"""Exact degree-one Johnson-map check for Magnus generators.
No theorem about degrees >=2 is asserted by this computation.
"""
from itertools import combinations

def multiply(a,b,degree=2):
    c={}
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=degree:
                w=u+v;c[w]=c.get(w,0)+x*y
    return {w:x for w,x in c.items() if x}

def expansion(word):
    out={():1}
    for s in word:
        i=abs(s)-1
        factor={():1,(i,):1} if s>0 else {():1,(i,):-1,(i,i):1}
        out=multiply(out,factor)
    return out

def generators(n):
    # Return (name, changed index, image word) with indices 0-based.
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i!=j:
                yield ('K',i,j),i,(j+1,i+1,-j-1)
        for j,k in combinations([j for j in range(n) if j!=i],2):
            yield ('C',i,j,k),i,(i+1,j+1,k+1,-j-1,-k-1)

def check(n):
    basis={(i,j,k) for i in range(n) for j,k in combinations(range(n),2)}
    images=[]
    for name,i,word in generators(n):
        d=expansion(word+(-i-1,));d[()]-=1
        d={u:x for u,x in d.items() if x}
        assert d and all(len(u)==2 for u in d)
        nonzero=[]
        for j,k in combinations(range(n),2):
            a=d.get((j,k),0)
            assert d.get((k,j),0)==-a
            if a: nonzero.append(((i,j,k),a))
        assert len(nonzero)==1
        key,sgn=nonzero[0]
        assert sgn in (-1,1)
        assert d=={(key[1],key[2]):sgn,(key[2],key[1]):-sgn}
        images.append(key)
    assert len(images)==len(set(images))==len(basis)==n*n*(n-1)//2
    assert set(images)==basis
    return len(images)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for n in range(2,13):
        print(n,check(n),'signed-permutation columns checked')
''')
exec(compile(p.read_text(),str(p),'exec'))

```

```
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion
from itertools import combinations,product
from sympy import Matrix,ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form

def red(w):
 s=[]
 for x in w:
  if s and s[-1]==-x:s.pop()
  else:s.append(x)
 return tuple(s)
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
def compose(f,g):
 return tuple(red(y for x in w for y in (f[x-1] if x>0 else inv(f[-x-1]))) for w in g)
def add(a,b,scale=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():a[w]=a.get(w,0)+scale*v
 return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if v}
def deriv(d,p):
 o={}
 for w,v in p.items():
  for t,i in enumerate(w):
   for u,x in d[i].items():
    key=w[:t]+u+w[t+1:];o[key]=o.get(key,0)+v*x
 return {w:v for w,v in o.items() if v}
def certificate(n):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  p=expansion(w+(-i-1,));p=add(p,{():1},-1)
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=p;ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=Matrix(cols).T
 print('n',n,'m',m,'bracket rank',B.rank(),'null',len(pairs)-B.rank(),flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):
  cand.append((g,{i:1},('g',i)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1},('p',i,j)))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1},('p',j,i)))
 R=[];seen=set();cert=[]
 for i,a in enumerate(gs):
  for b,v,name in cand:
   if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
   r=[0]*len(pairs)
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x
   t=tuple(r)
   if any(r) and t not in seen:
    seen.add(t);R.append(r);cert.append((i,name))
 M=Matrix(R).T
 print('relations',len(R),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.shape)) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF',diag,flush=True)
 return B,M,cert
if __name__=='__main__':certificate(3)

```

```
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)

  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident)

  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]

   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])

 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/degree2_certificate.py').write_text('''"""An exact integral proof certificate for degree-2 injectivity for IA_3.
Dependencies: Magnus generation and the degree-one Johnson homomorphism.
Relations used are genuine commuting pairs and their Nielsen conjugates.
"""
import sys
from itertools import combinations,product,permutations
from sympy import Matrix,ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion

def red(w):
 s=[]
 for x in w:
  if s and s[-1]==-x:s.pop()
  else:s.append(x)
 return tuple(s)
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
def compose(f,g):
 return tuple(red(y for x in w for y in (f[x-1] if x>0 else inv(f[-x-1]))) for w in g)
def add(a,b,scale=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():a[w]=a.get(w,0)+scale*v
 return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if v}
def deriv(d,p):
 o={}
 for w,v in p.items():
  for t,i in enumerate(w):
   for u,x in d[i].items():
    key=w[:t]+u+w[t+1:];o[key]=o.get(key,0)+v*x
 return {w:v for w,v in o.items() if v}
def certificate(n=3):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  p=add(expansion(w+(-i-1,)),{():1},-1)
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=p;ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=Matrix(cols).T
 rankB=B.rank()
 print('n',n,'m',m,'bracket rank',rankB,'null',len(pairs)-rankB,flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):cand.append((g,{i:1},('g',i)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1},('p',i,j)))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1},('p',j,i)))
 R=[];seen=set();cert=[]
 for (a,u,namea),(b,v,nameb) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  r=[0]*len(pairs)
  for i,y in u.items():
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x*y
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x*y
  t=tuple(r)
  if any(r) and t not in seen:
   seen.add(t);R.append(r);cert.append((namea,nameb))
 M=Matrix(R).T
 print('relations',len(R),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 keys=[];signs=[]
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)
  w=next(w for w in D[k] if w[0]<w[1])
  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident)
  t[ii]=(ii+1,jj+1);ti[ii]=(ii+1,-jj-1)
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]
  for g in gs:
   h=compose(t,compose(g,ti))
   hd=[add(expansion(h[k]+(-k-1,)),{():1},-1) for k in range(n)]
   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])
  A=Matrix(C).T
  W=Matrix([[A[i,p]*A[j,q]-A[i,q]*A[j,p] for p,q in pairs] for i,j in pairs])
  actions.append(W)
 M=Matrix.hstack(M,*[W*M for W in actions])
 rankM=M.rank()
 print('GL-closure relations',M.cols,'rank',rankM,flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.shape)) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF',diag,flush=True)
 assert rankM==len(pairs)-rankB
 assert all(x==1 for x in diag)
 print('PASS: actual group relations equal the full integral bracket kernel')
 return B,M,cert

if __name__=='__main__':certificate(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from degree2_certificate import certificate
certificate(3)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/degree2_sage_certificate.py').write_text('''"""Sage/FLINT implementation of the exact degree-two lattice certificate."""
import sys
from itertools import combinations,permutations,product
from sage.all import matrix,ZZ
from degree2_certificate import red,inv,compose,add,deriv
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion

def certificate(n):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=add(expansion(w+(-i-1,)),{():1},-1);ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));N=len(pairs);bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose();rankB=B.rank();target=N-rankB
 print('n',n,'bracket rank',rankB,'null',target,flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):cand.append((g,{i:1}))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1}))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1}))
 R=[];seen=set()
 for i,a in enumerate(gs):
  for b,v in cand:
   if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
   r=[0]*N
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x
   t=tuple(r)
   if any(r) and t not in seen:seen.add(t);R.append(r)
 H=matrix(ZZ,R).hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False)
 print('initial',len(R),'rank',H.nrows(),flush=True)
 assert B*H.transpose()==0
 keys=[];signs=[]
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)
  w=next(w for w in D[k] if w[0]<w[1])
  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident);t[ii]=(ii+1,jj+1);ti[ii]=(ii+1,-jj-1)
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]
  for g in gs:
   h=compose(t,compose(g,ti));hd=[add(expansion(h[k]+(-k-1,)),{():1},-1) for k in range(n)]
   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])
  A=matrix(ZZ,C).transpose();W={}
  for col,(p,q) in enumerate(pairs):
   u={i:A[i,p] for i in range(m) if A[i,p]};v={j:A[j,q] for j in range(m) if A[j,q]}
   for i,x in u.items():
    for j,y in v.items():
     if i<j:key=(bp[i,j],col);W[key]=W.get(key,0)+x*y
     elif i>j:key=(bp[j,i],col);W[key]=W.get(key,0)-x*y
  actions.append(matrix(ZZ,N,N,W,sparse=True).transpose())
 for roundno in range(1,5):
  old=H
  for t,W in enumerate(actions):
   HW=H*W
   H=H.stack(HW).hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False)
   assert B*H.transpose()==0
   print('round',roundno,'action',t,'rank',H.nrows(),flush=True)
  if H==old:break
  if H.nrows()==target:break
 diag=H.elementary_divisors();print('rank',H.nrows(),'elementary divisors',set(diag),flush=True)
 assert H.nrows()==target and all(x==1 for x in diag)
 print('PASS exact primitive true-relation lattice equals bracket kernel')
 return B,H

if __name__=='__main__':certificate(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from degree2_sage_certificate import certificate
certificate(5)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_allrank_degree2_audit.md').write_text(r'''# All-rank degree-two injectivity: weight classification audit

Uses the exact rank-3/rank-4 lattice certificate and the two universal
relations proved in `agent4_degree2_relations.md`.
Write a(i;jk) = e_i^* tensor [X_j,X_k], with j != k and antisymmetry
in j,k. Each is the signed Johnson image of one Magnus generator.
Call it K-type if i is j or k, and C-type otherwise. K-type has
weight e_t; C-type has weight e_j+e_k-e_i.

The abelian group Lambda^2(V) is weight graded. All relations below
are genuine relations in gr_2(IA_n). Its bracket map is integral.
For <=4 labels, any relation with zero derivation image is a genuine
relation by the exact n=4 certificate (and n=2/3 results).

Universal relations:
1. Same-target C-type pairs vanish in gr_2 (partial-inner identity).
2. Dummy collapse: a(i;pk) wedge a(p;qr) equals
   a(i;qk) wedge a(q;qr) when the initial and cross terms commute.
   This follows by shearing X_p -> X_p+X_q in the commuting relation
   a(i;pk) wedge a(q;qr)=0 and subtracting commuting terms.
   Besides the five-distinct-label case, it remains valid when k=i,
   provided i,p,q,r are distinct. If k=q choose r instead of q.

## Weights with a negative entry
Two distinct negative entries: neither target occurs in the opposite
tail, so the automorphisms commute exactly. An entry -2 means two
C-types with the same target, covered by relation 1.

Exactly one negative entry -e_i leaves three positive output labels
(counted with multiplicity). The derivation image has only target i
and is an integral degree-three free Lie element on those labels.
Any intermediate contracted target outside these labels is eliminated
by dummy collapse. Of q,r at least one differs from k because q!=r,
so an allowed collapse always exists. All remaining columns of any
fixed weight are supported on i plus the positive labels, at most four
labels. Consequently every kernel relation is a local rank<=4 relation.

## Weight e_a+e_b with a!=b
For each s outside {a,b}, put
 U_s=e_s^* [[s,a],b], V_s=e_s^* [[s,b],a],
 Z_a=e_a^* [a,[a,b]], Z_b=e_b^* [[a,b],b].
Use the following actual degree-two bracket columns:
 W_s=a(s;sa) wedge a(s;sb), image U_s-V_s;
 L_s=a(s;ab) wedge a(a;as), image U_s+Z_a;
 G=a(a;ab) wedge a(b;ab), image -Z_a+Z_b.
Their images are integrally independent: inspecting U_s,V_s forces
all W_s,L_s coefficients to vanish, and then inspecting Z_b kills G.

All K-K pairs either commute exactly or have support<=3.
A C-K pair must be a(i;ab) wedge K(p;i). If p lies outside {a,b,i},
dummy collapse (the allowed k=target case) replaces it by a bracket
on {a,b,p}; otherwise it already has support<=3.
A C-C pair must be
 E_ip=a(i;pa) wedge a(p;ib), with i,p outside {a,b} and i!=p.
Its image is -V_i+U_p, hence by rank4 injectivity
 E_ip=-L_i+L_p+W_i.
Every column supported on {a,b,s} has image in the primitive lattice
spanned by W_s,L_s,G: their 3 columns have a unit 3x3 minor in the
4-dimensional coordinate group (U_s,V_s,Z_a,Z_b). They span the
rank3 bracket image there, by the certified rank3 image computation.
Thus every column reduces integrally to W_s,L_s,G, whose independent
images prove injectivity.

## Weight 2e_a
K-K pairs commute (same conjugator). C-K is impossible because the
C-type has two distinct positive output labels. The only C-C columns
are E_ip=a(i;pa) wedge a(p;ia), i,p!=a, i!=p.
Put T_i=e_i^* [[i,a],a]. Their images are T_p-T_i.
Fix t!=a; the columns E_it (i!=a,t) have independent images.
Every E_ip=E_it+E_tp follows from rank4 injectivity on {a,i,p,t}.
Thus there is no kernel in this weight either.

These exhaust weights (sum of coordinates is two), proving
ker[Lambda^2(V)->Der_2]=relations in gr_2(IA_n), for every n.
Consequently gamma_2(IA_n) intersect A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n).
Together with the degree-one theorem this also gives A_3=gamma_3.
''')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions.py').write_text('''"""Exact free-group checks of Magnus-generator expressions for Aut conjugation."""
import sys,json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from degree1_certificate import generators

def magnus(n):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];gis=[];names=[]
 for name,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g));names.append(name)
  if name[0]=='K':
   j=name[2];wi=(-j-1,i+1,j+1)
  else:
   j,k=name[2:];wi=(i+1,k+1,j+1,-k-1,-j-1)
  gi=list(ident);gi[i]=wi;gis.append(tuple(gi))
  assert compose(g,gi)==ident and compose(gi,g)==ident
 return ident,gs,gis,names

def expressions(n=3,radius=3):
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(n);m=len(gs)
 letters=[(i+1,g,gis[i]) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]+[(-i-1,gis[i],g) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]
 table={ident:((),ident)};frontier=[ident]
 for depth in range(1,radius+1):
  new=[]
  for f in frontier:
   w,fi=table[f]
   for x,g,gi in letters:
    if w and w[-1]==-x:continue
    h=compose(f,g)
    if h in table:continue
    table[h]=(w+(x,),compose(gi,fi));new.append(h)
  frontier=new;print('radius',depth,'size',len(table),flush=True)
 # Standard elementary transvection, swap, inversion, cycle.
 acts=[]
 t=list(ident);ti=list(ident);t[0]=(1,2);ti[0]=(1,-2)
 acts.append(('T',tuple(t),tuple(ti)))
 t=list(ident);t[0],t[1]=t[1],t[0];acts.append(('P',tuple(t),tuple(t)))
 t=list(ident);t[0]=(-1,);acts.append(('I',tuple(t),tuple(t)))
 cyc=tuple(((i+1)%n+1,) for i in range(n));cyci=tuple(((i-1)%n+1,) for i in range(n))
 acts.append(('C',cyc,cyci))
 result={}
 def evaluate(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 for name,t,ti in acts:
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  words=[]
  for k,g in enumerate(gs):
   target=compose(t,compose(g,ti));answer=None
   if target in table:answer=table[target][0]
   else:
    for b,(bw,bi) in table.items():
     a=compose(target,bi)
     if a in table:
      answer=red(table[a][0]+bw);break
   if answer is None:raise ValueError(('not found',name,k))
   assert evaluate(answer)==target
   words.append(answer)
   print('action',name,'generator',k+1,'word',answer,flush=True)
  result[name]={'auto':t,'inverse':ti,'words':words}
 data={'n':n,'generators':gs,'inverses':gis,'names':names,'actions':result}
 Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n%d.json'%n).write_text(json.dumps(data))
 print('All conjugation words checked exactly',flush=True)
 return data
if __name__=='__main__':expressions(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
''')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words.py').write_text('''"""Exact words for Magnus generators in T,P,I,C Aut generators."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red

def build(n=3):
 d=json.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n%d.json'%n).read_text())
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));act=[d['actions'][s] for s in ['T','P','I','C']]
 aa=[tuple(map(tuple,a['auto'])) for a in act];ai=[tuple(map(tuple,a['inverse'])) for a in act]
 def evaluate(w):
  out=ident
  for x in w:out=compose(out,aa[x-1] if x>0 else ai[-x-1])
  return out
 # Permutations generated by P,C. Keep only shortest representative for each pair (image0,image1).
 table={ident:()};front=[ident]
 while front:
  new=[]
  for p in front:
   for x in (2,4):
    q=compose(p,aa[x-1])
    if q not in table:table[q]=table[p]+(x,);new.append(q)
  front=new
  if len(table)>50000:raise ValueError('too many permutations; use n<=8')
 pairs={}
 for p,w in table.items():pairs.setdefault((p[0][0]-1,p[1][0]-1),w)
 def R(i,j):
  w=pairs[i,j];return red(w+(1,)+inv(w))
 def I(i):
  w=next(w for p,w in table.items() if p[0]==(i+1,));return red(w+(3,)+inv(w))
 result=[]
 for nm,g in zip(d['names'],d['generators']):
  if nm[0]=='K':
   i,j=nm[1:];ii=I(i);rr=R(i,j);w=red(ii+inv(rr)+ii+inv(rr))
  else:
   i,j,k=nm[1:];a,b=R(i,j),R(i,k);w=red(a+b+inv(a)+inv(b))
  assert evaluate(w)==tuple(map(tuple,g)),(nm,w,evaluate(w),g)
  result.append(w)
 out={'n':n,'action_order':['T','P','I','C'],'words':result,'names':d['names']}
 Path('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words_n%d.json'%n).write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('checked',len(result),'Magnus generator Aut words, rank',n)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':build(3);build(4)
''')
```

```
  for i in w:q=mul(q,L[i],3)
  out=add(out,q)
 return out

def action_matrix(words):
 L=[];Q=[]
 for w in words:
  ex=expand(w);li={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==1}
  f2={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2}
  qi=add({t:2*x for t,x in f2.items()},mul(li,li,3),-1)
  for (i,),x in li.items():qi=add(qi,{(i,i):x})
  coords2(qi)
  L.append(li);Q.append(qi)
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  a=coords2(br(L[i],L[j]))
  b=coords(add(br(Q[i],L[j]),br(L[i],Q[j])))
  cols.append(a+b)
 for p in LP:cols.append([0]*len(pairs)+coords(linear_sub(p,L)))
 return Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose()

def row_basis(M):
 return M.hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False)

def main(collision_file=None):
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 # Audit supplied action words against their exact automorphisms.
 acts=[]
 for name,d in data['actions'].items():
  words=[tuple(w) for w in d['words']]
  # The source file independently checks these; audit again here.
  ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(N))
  ig=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in data['inverses']]
  def ev(w):
   f=ident

   return f
  t=tuple(map(tuple,d['auto']));ti=tuple(map(tuple,d['inverse']))
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  for g,w in zip(G,words):assert ev(w)==compose(t,compose(g,ti))
  A=action_matrix(words);acts.append(A)
  print('built and audited action',name,flush=True)
 rel=commuting_relators()
 init=[initial_vector(ex) for w,ex in rel]
 if collision_file:
  for w in json.load(open(collision_file)):
   f=ident
   for t in w:f=compose(f,G[t-1] if t>0 else ig[-t-1])
   assert f==ident
   init.append(initial_vector(expand(w)))
  print('audited collision relators',len(init)-len(rel),flush=True)
 H=row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,init))
 print('initial relation lattice rank',H.nrows(),flush=True)
 for r in range(1,9):
  parts=[H]+[H*A.transpose() for A in acts]
  extra=[]
  for z in H.rows():
   r2={}
   for x,q in zip(z[:len(pairs)],L2):r2=add(r2,q,x)
   if not r2:continue
   for i in range(m):extra.append([0]*len(pairs)+[2*x for x in coords(br({(i,):1},r2))])
  if extra:parts.append(Matrix(ZZ,extra))
  newH=row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,[list(z) for P in parts for z in P.rows()]))
  print('closure',r,'rank',newH.nrows(),flush=True)
  if H==newH:break
  H=newH
 R2=H[:,:len(pairs)]
 K=R2.transpose().right_kernel().basis_matrix()
 C=K*H[:,len(pairs):]
 assert all(x%2==0 for x in C.list())
 C=C.apply_map(lambda x:x//2)
 C=row_basis(C)
 print('quadratic rank',R2.rank(),'cubic relation rank',C.nrows(),flush=True)
 # Compute exact target matrix in Lyndon coordinates.
 keys=[(i,w) for i in range(N) for w in product(range(N),repeat=4)]
 B=Matrix(ZZ,[[ld(w)[i].get(t,0) for w in L3] for i,t in keys])
 assert B*C.transpose()==0
 S=C.smith_form(transformation=False)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.dimensions())) if S[i,i]]
 print('Johnson rank',B.rank(),'kernel',len(L3)-B.rank(),'SNF counts',{int(x):diag.count(x) for x in set(diag)},flush=True)
 if C.nrows()==len(L3)-B.rank() and all(x==1 for x in diag):print('PASS: degree3 IA3 integral Johnson injectivity')
 else:print('INCOMPLETE: no injectivity claim')
 return H,C,B
```

```
"""Exact exploratory degree-three relation computation for IA3.
Every relation column is justified by an actual exact commuting identity.
PASS only if full primitive Johnson kernel is obtained.
"""
from itertools import product, combinations
from functools import lru_cache
from sage.all import Matrix, ZZ
from degree1_certificate import generators
from degree2_certificate import red, inv, compose

N=3
G=[]
for name,i,w in generators(N):
 f=[(j+1,) for j in range(N)];f[i]=w;G.append(tuple(f))
m=len(G)

def mul(a,b,lim=3):
 out={}
 for u,x in a.items():
  for v,y in b.items():
   if len(u)+len(v)<=lim:out[u+v]=out.get(u+v,0)+x*y
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def add(a,b,k=1):
 out=a.copy()
 for w,x in b.items():out[w]=out.get(w,0)+k*x
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def br(a,b):return add(mul(a,b,20),mul(b,a,20),-1)
def expand(w):
 a={():1}
 for s in w:
  i=abs(s)-1
  f={():1,(i,):1} if s>0 else {tuple([i]*j):(-1)**j for j in range(4)}
  a=mul(a,f)
 return a

def commuting_relators():
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(G):cand.append((g,(i+1,)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(G[i],G[j]),(i+1,j+1)))
  cand.append((compose(G[j],G[i]),(j+1,i+1)))
 seen=set();out=[]
 for (a,u),(b,v) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  w=red(u+v+inv(u)+inv(v));ex=expand(w)
  if any(len(t)==1 and x for t,x in ex.items()):raise ValueError('bad')
  key=tuple(sorted((t,x) for t,x in ex.items() if 2<=len(t)<=3))
  if not key or key in seen:continue
  seen.add(key);out.append((w,ex))
 return out

def lyndon(w):return bool(w) and all(w < w[j:]+w[:j] for j in range(1,len(w)))
@lru_cache(None)
def lp(w):
 if len(w)==1:return {w:1}
 j=next(j for j in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[j:]))
 return br(lp(w[:j]),lp(w[j:]))
L3=[w for w in product(range(m),repeat=3) if lyndon(w)]
LP=[lp(w) for w in L3]
def coords(p):
 p=p.copy();v=[]
 for w,q in zip(L3,LP):
  x=p.get(w,0);v.append(x)
  if x:p=add(p,q,-x)
 assert not p,('not free Lie',p)
 return v

def deriv(d,p):
 out={}
 for w,x in p.items():
  for j,t in enumerate(w):
   for v,y in d[t].items():out[w[:j]+v+w[j+1:]]=out.get(w[:j]+v+w[j+1:],0)+x*y
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def dbr(d,e):return tuple(add(deriv(d,e[i]),deriv(e,d[i]),-1) for i in range(N))
Ds=[]
for name,i,w in generators(N):
 # Calculate degree-two displacement using the same generic Magnus routine.
 ex=expand(w+(-i-1,));d=[{} for _ in range(N)]
 d[i]={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2};Ds.append(tuple(d))
@lru_cache(None)
def ld(w):
 if len(w)==1:return Ds[w[0]]
 j=next(j for j in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[j:]))
 return dbr(ld(w[:j]),ld(w[j:]))

def main():
 rel=commuting_relators();print('actual commuting relators',len(rel),flush=True)
 pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2))
 R2=Matrix(ZZ,[[ex.get((i,j),0) for w,ex in rel] for i,j in pairs])
 K=R2.right_kernel().basis_matrix();print('R2 rank',R2.rank(),'integer syzygies',K.nrows(),flush=True)
 cols=[]
 for w,ex in rel:
  q={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2}
  for i in range(m):cols.append(coords(br({(i,):1},q)))
 for z in K.rows():
  p={}
  for x,(_,ex) in zip(z,rel):
   if x:p=add(p,{t:y for t,y in ex.items() if len(t)==3},x)
  if p:cols.append(coords(p))
 M=Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose();print('cubic relation matrix',M.dimensions(),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 keys=[(i,w) for i in range(N) for w in product(range(N),repeat=4)]
 B=Matrix(ZZ,[[ld(w)[i].get(t,0) for w in L3] for i,t in keys])
 print('domain',len(L3),'Johnson rank',B.rank(),'kernel',len(L3)-B.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==0
 H=M.hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False) if False else None
 S=M.smith_form(transformation=False)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.dimensions())) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF counts',{int(x):diag.count(x) for x in set(diag)},flush=True)
 if len(diag)==len(L3)-B.rank() and all(x==1 for x in diag):
  print('PASS: full integral degree3 Johnson kernel generated by verified relations')
 else:print('INCOMPLETE relation lattice; no injectivity claim')
 return B,M,rel
if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

```
import json

def generate():

 for name in order:maps.append([add(expand(w),{():1},-1) for w in d['actions'][name]['words']])
 @lru_cache(None)
 def subword(a,w):
  p={():1}

  return p
 def apply(a,p):
  out={}
  for w,x in p.items():out=add(out,subword(a,w),x)
  return out
 # Positive words for inverses of T,P,I,C. I_1=P I P.
 inverse={1:(2,3,2,1,2,3,2),2:(2,),3:(3,),4:(4,4)}

 ai=[tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['inverse'])) for s in order]

 for i,w in inverse.items():assert ev(w)==ai[i-1]

  print('Peiffer block',gi+1,'done',flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_peiffer_degree3.json').write_text(json.dumps(rel))
 Path('workspace/agent2_peiffer_degree3_vectors.json').write_text(json.dumps([[int(x) for x in v] for v in vectors]))
 print('81 true relator vectors rank',Matrix(ZZ,vectors).rank(),flush=True)
```

```
        fi;
```

```
    local G, p, cl, ngens, collector, qs, t, noninteractive;
    if Length( arg ) < 2 or Length( arg ) >= 6 then
        Error( "PQuotient( <G>, <p>, <cl>", " [, <ngens>] [, \"single\" | \"combinatorial\" ] )" );
    fi;
    G := arg[1];
    if not IsFpGroup( G ) then
        Error( "The first argument must be a finitely presented group" );
    fi;
    p := arg[2];
    if not (IsInt( p ) and p > 0 and IsPrime( p )) then
        Error( "The second argument must be a positive prime" );
    fi;
    cl := 666;

    collector := "combinatorial";
    if Length( arg ) >= 3 then
        cl := arg[3];
        if not (IsInt( 3 ) and cl > 0) then
            Error( "The third argument (if present) is the p-class and", " must be a positive integer" );
        fi;
    fi;
    if Length( arg ) >= 4 then
        if IsInt( arg[4] ) then
            ngens := arg[4];
            if not ngens > 0 then
                Error( "If the fourth argument is present and an integer,", " then it is the initial number of generators in the", " collector and must be a positive integer" );
            fi;
        elif IsString( arg[4] ) then
            collector := arg[4];
            if not (collector = "single" or collector = "combinatorial") then
                Error( "If the fourth argument is present and a string", " then it is the collector that is used during the", " p-quotient algorithm and must be either ", " \"single\" or \"combinatorial\"" );
            fi;
        fi;
    fi;
    if Length( arg ) >= 5 then
        collector := arg[5];
        if not (IsString( collector ) and (collector = "single" or collector = "combinatorial")) then
            Error( "If the fifth argument is present and a string", " then it is the collector that is used during the", " p-quotient algorithm and must be either ", " \"single\" or \"combinatorial\"" );
        fi;
    fi;
    noninteractive := ValueOption( "noninteractive" ) = true;
    ClearPQuotientStatistics(  );

    Info( InfoQuotientSystem, 1, "Class ", LengthOfDescendingSeries( qs ) + 1, " quotient" );
    t := Runtime(  );
    AbelianPQuotient( qs );
    Info( InfoQuotientSystem, 1, "  rank of this layer: ", RanksOfDescendingSeries( qs )[LengthOfDescendingSeries( qs )], " (runtime: ", Runtime(  ) - t, " msec)" );
    while LengthOfDescendingSeries( qs ) < cl do
        t := Runtime(  );
        Info( InfoQuotientSystem, 1, "Class ", LengthOfDescendingSeries( qs ) + 1, " quotient" );
        Info( InfoQuotientSystem, 2, "  Define new generators." );
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_tail_relators.py').write_text('''"""Exact partial-inner / conjugated-tail relations in IA3."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from itertools import product
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import G,expand,compose,inv,red
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector

def comm(a,b):return red(a+b+inv(a)+inv(b))
def generate():
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'));idx={tuple(nm):i+1 for i,nm in enumerate(d['names'])}
 gi=[tuple(map(tuple,g)) for g in d['inverses']];ident=((1,),(2,),(3,))
 def evaluate(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,G[x-1] if x>0 else gi[-x-1])
  return f
 rel=[];vectors=[]
 for i in range(3):
  j,k=[x for x in range(3) if x!=i]
  C=(idx['C',i,j,k],);Pj=(idx['K',k,j],);Pk=(idx['K',j,k],);Pu=comm(Pj,Pk)
  for a,b in product(range(-2,3),repeat=2):
   pw=(Pj*a if a>=0 else inv(Pj)*(-a))+(Pk*b if b>=0 else inv(Pk)*(-b))
   Tv=red(pw+C+inv(pw));r=red(comm(C,Tv)+inv(comm(Pu,Tv)))
   assert evaluate(r)==ident,(i,a,b,r)
   rel.append(r);vectors.append(initial_vector(expand(r)))
 Path('workspace/agent2_tail_relator_words.json').write_text(json.dumps(rel))
 Path('workspace/agent2_tail_relator_vectors.json').write_text(json.dumps([[int(x) for x in v] for v in vectors]))
 print('exacttailrelators',len(rel),'rank',Matrix(ZZ,vectors).rank())
 return rel,vectors
if __name__=='__main__':generate()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_tail_relators import generate
generate()
```

```
  2 log(r)_3 = 2 r_3(e) + sum_(i,j) c_ij(e_i^2 e_j+e_i e_j^2),

`agent1_degree3_closure.py`. The Lyndon coefficient functions verify
that both displayed homogeneous components lie in the integral free Lie
lattice after the indicated doubling.

l=f_1(e) and q=2 f_2(e)-l^2+sum_i coeff_(X_i)(f_1)e_i^2. Then
log(w)=l+q/2+terms of degree>=3. Therefore an endomorphism sending
x_i to w_i acts on the displayed coordinates by

 [e_i,e_j] -> [l_i,l_j] in degree2,
             [q_i,l_j]+[l_i,q_j] in doubled degree3;
 2r_3      -> (2r_3)(l_1,...,l_m).

gamma_4; hence the logarithms of their products are additive through
degree3. Integral kernel combinations of quadratic coordinates therefore
produce the exact cubic relations contributed by these true relators.
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_rs_loops.py').write_text('''"""Finite exact Reidemeister-Schreier chunks; no completeness claim."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus

def mat(f):return tuple(sum((1 if x==i+1 else -1 if x==-i-1 else 0) for x in f[j]) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))
def mm(a,b):return tuple(sum(a[3*i+k]*b[3*k+j] for k in range(3)) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))

def run(radius=4,mitm=3):
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_AutF3_presentation.json'))
 A=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in ar['aut_images']];AI=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in ar['inverse_images']]
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(3);mi=mat(ident)
 letters=[(i+1,g,AI[i],mat(g)) for i,g in enumerate(A)]+[(-i-1,AI[i],g,mat(AI[i])) for i,g in enumerate(A)]
 # Short GL transversal, with exact Aut lifts and inverse lifts.
 trans={mi:((),ident,ident)};front=[mi]
 for depth in range(1,radius+1):
  new=[]
  for M in front:
   w,f,fi=trans[M]
   for x,g,gi,Mg in letters:
    Q=mm(M,Mg)
    if Q in trans:continue
    trans[Q]=(w+(x,),compose(f,g),compose(gi,fi));new.append(Q)
  front=new;print('GLball',depth,len(trans),flush=True)
 # Magnus radius4 table.
 ll=[(i+1,g,gis[i]) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]+[(-i-1,gis[i],g) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]
 tab={ident:((),ident)};front=[ident];short=[]
 for depth in range(1,5):
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   w,fi=tab[f]
   for x,g,gi in ll:
    if w and w[-1]==-x:continue
    h=compose(f,g)
    if h in tab:continue
    tab[h]=(w+(x,),compose(gi,fi));new.append(h)
  front=new
  if depth<=mitm:short+=new
 print('IA radius4',len(tab),'complements',len(short),flush=True)
 cache={};fail=set();relations=[]
 def express(f):
  if f in cache:return cache[f]
  if f in fail:return None
  if f in tab:cache[f]=tab[f][0];return cache[f]
  for b in short:
   bw,bi=tab[b];a=compose(f,bi)
   if a in tab:
    cache[f]=red(tab[a][0]+bw);return cache[f]
  fail.add(f);return None
 def ev(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 def al(x):return A[x-1] if x>0 else AI[-x-1]
 def ali(x):return AI[x-1] if x>0 else A[-x-1]
 # Build missing transversal vertices consistently from actual loop prefixes.
 loops=[tuple(reversed(r)) for r in ar['relators']]
 for r in loops:
  M=mi;f=ident;fi=ident;w=()
  for x in r:
   M=mm(M,mat(al(x)));f=compose(f,al(x));fi=compose(ali(x),fi);w=w+(x,)
   if M not in trans:trans[M]=(w,f,fi)
  assert f==ident and M==mi
 for ri,r in enumerate(loops):
  M=mi;out=();okay=True
  for pos,x in enumerate(r):
   Q=mm(M,mat(al(x)));f=compose(trans[M][1],compose(al(x),trans[Q][2]))
   assert mat(f)==mi
   w=express(f)
   if w is None:
    print('loop',ri,'edge',pos,'FAIL autlength',sum(map(len,f)),flush=True);okay=False;break
   out=red(out+w);M=Q
  if okay:
   assert ev(out)==ident
   if out:relations.append(out)
   print('loop',ri,'SUCCESS IAwordlength',len(out),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_rs_relator_words.json').write_text(json.dumps(relations))
 print('Saved',len(relations),'exact RS identities; cache',len(cache),'fails',len(fail),flush=True)
 return relations
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_rs_loops import run
run()
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_rs_target_search.py').write_text('''"""Target short Magnus expressions at long RS-completion edge expressions."""
import json,pickle
from pathlib import Path
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from agent1_degree3 import expand
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ

def run(depth=5):
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(3)
 cache=pickle.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_rs_edgecache.pkl').read_bytes())
 letters=[(i+1,g) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]+[(-i-1,gis[i]) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]
 tab={ident:()};front=[ident]
 rel=[];seen=set();matches=0
 for dep in range(1,depth+1):
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   w=tab[f]
   for x,g in letters:
    if w and w[-1]==-x:continue
    h=compose(f,g);ww=w+(x,)
    if h in cache and len(cache[h])>len(ww):
     r=red(cache[h]+inv(ww));v=tuple(initial_vector(expand(r)));matches+=1
     if any(v) and v not in seen:seen.add(v);rel.append((r,v))
    if dep<depth and h not in tab:tab[h]=ww;new.append(h)
  front=new
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_edge_preimage.py').write_text('''"""Canonical IA3 edge lifts modulo gamma3, using certified c<=2 equality."""
import pickle
from pathlib import Path
from itertools import combinations,product
from sage.all import Matrix,vector,ZZ
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from agent1_degree3 import expand,Ds,dbr

ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(3);pairs=list(combinations(range(9),2))
B=Matrix(ZZ,[[dbr(Ds[i],Ds[j])[k].get(w,0) for i,j in pairs] for k in range(3) for w in product(range(3),repeat=3)])
D,U,V=B.smith_form();rank=B.rank();assert rank==18
assert all(D[i,i]==1 for i in range(rank))
keys=[];sign=[]
for d in Ds:
 k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(d) if p);w=next(w for w in d[k] if w[0]<w[1]);keys.append((k,w));sign.append(d[k][w])

def evaluate(w):
 f=ident
 for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
 return f

def powerword(w,n):return w*n if n>=0 else inv(w)*(-n)

def preimage(f):
 """Return a Magnus word w such that f*w^-1 is in A3=gamma3(IA3)."""
 f=tuple(map(tuple,f));ex=[expand(f[k]+(-k-1,)) for k in range(3)]
 assert not any(len(w)==1 for p in ex for w in p)
 aa=[ex[k].get(w,0)*s for (k,w),s in zip(keys,sign)]
 w1=tuple(x for i,a in enumerate(aa) for x in powerword((i+1,),a))
 # Residual r=f*w1^-1 is in A2=gamma2; compute its true tau2.
 r=compose(f,evaluate(inv(w1)));rx=[expand(r[k]+(-k-1,)) for k in range(3)]
 assert not any(len(w) in (1,2) for p in rx for w in p)
 y=vector(ZZ,[rx[k].get(w,0) for k in range(3) for w in product(range(3),repeat=3)])
 z=U*y
 assert all(x==0 for x in z[rank:])
 xx=V*vector(ZZ,list(z[:rank])+[0]*(36-rank));assert B*xx==y
 w2=()
 for (i,j),a in zip(pairs,xx):
  if a:w2+=powerword((i+1,j+1,-i-1,-j-1),int(a))
 w=red(w2+w1)
 return w

if __name__=='__main__':
 cache=pickle.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_rs_merged_edgecache.pkl').read_bytes())
 for f in list(cache)[:50]:
  w=preimage(f)
  r=compose(f,evaluate(inv(w)))
  assert all(not any(1<=len(t)<=3 for t in expand(r[k]+(-k-1,))) for k in range(3))
 print('PASS 50 actualSchreieredges matched moduloA3=gamma3')
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import runpy
runpy.run_path('workspace/agent2_edge_preimage.py',run_name='__main__')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_crossed_cover_seeds.py').write_text('''"""Exact finite invariant L-presentation seeds for a central IA3 cover.
No claim that this cover equals IA3. Finite words are generated exactly.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import red,inv

def generate(limit=2000000):
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 wd=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words_n3.json'))
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_AutF3_presentation.json'))
 order=['T','P','I','C'];maps=[[tuple(w) for w in d['actions'][s]['words']] for s in order]
 imaps=[[inv(w) for w in a] for a in maps]
 def apply(a,w):
  out=red(y for x in w for y in (maps[a][x-1] if x>0 else imaps[a][-x-1]))
  if len(out)>limit:raise OverflowError(('wordcap',a,len(out)))
  return out
 def image(aw,h):
  w=(h+1,)
  for a in reversed(aw):w=apply(a-1,w)
  return w
 inverse={1:(2,3,2,1,2,3,2),2:(2,),3:(3,),4:(4,4)}
 peiffer=[]
 for g,ww in enumerate(wd['words']):
  aw=tuple(y for x in ww for y in ((x,) if x>0 else inverse[-x]))
  for h in range(9):peiffer.append(red(image(aw,h)+(g+1,-h-1,-g-1)))
  print('Peifferblock',g,'maxlength',max(map(len,peiffer)),flush=True)
 # Aut relation loops, GAP right action is reversed.
 I0=(3,);I1=(4,3,4,4);I2=(4,4,3,4)
 S=I0+I1+I2+(4,);T=(3,2);U=(1,)
 apos={1:S,2:T,3:U};ainv={1:S*5,2:T*3,3:inverse[1]}
 aut=[]
 for j,raw in enumerate(ar['relators']):
  aw=tuple(y for x in reversed(raw) for y in (apos[x] if x>0 else ainv[-x]))
  for h in range(9):aut.append(red(image(aw,h)+(-h-1,)))
  print('Autblock',j,'maxlength',max(map(len,aut)),flush=True)
 for aw in [(2,2),(3,3),(4,4,4),(1,2,3,2,1,2,3,2)]:
  for h in range(9):aut.append(red(image(aw,h)+(-h-1,)))
 out={'n':3,'endomorphisms':maps,'peiffer':peiffer,'aut_relations':aut}
 Path('workspace/agent2_crossed_cover_seeds.json').write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('Savedfullcoverseeds',len(peiffer)+len(aut),'totalletters',sum(map(len,peiffer+aut)),flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':generate()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_crossed_cover_seeds import generate
generate()
```

```
 p=Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n%d.json'%n);d=json.loads(p.read_text());m=len(d['generators'])
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(m);gens=libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F);out={}
 for name in ['T','I','P','C']:
  ims=[]
  for w in d['actions'][name]['words']:
   q=libgap.One(F)
   for x in w:q=q*(gens[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1))
   ims.append(q)
  hom=libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,F,gens,ims)
  assert bool(libgap.IsBijective(hom))
  ih=libgap.InverseGeneralMapping(hom)
  words=[[int(x) for x in libgap.LetterRepAssocWord(libgap.Image(ih,g))] for g in gens]
  # Literal free-group-word checks, not just GAP's IsBijective.
  def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
  def red(w):
   a=[]
   for x in w:
    if a and a[-1]==-x:a.pop()
    else:a.append(x)
   return tuple(a)
  fw=d['actions'][name]['words']
  def sub(w,mp):return red(y for x in w for y in (mp[x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[-x-1])))
  for i in range(m):assert sub(words[i],fw)==(i+1,) and sub(fw[i],words)==(i+1,)
  out[name]=words
  print('n',n,name,'inversemaxlength',max(map(len,words)),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n%d.json'%n).write_text(json.dumps(out))
```

```
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,red,inv

def generate(limit=2000000):
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'))
 wd=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words_n3.json'))
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_AutF3_four_generator_presentation.json'))
 order=['T','P','I','C'];mp={};aa={};ident=((1,),(2,),(3,))
 for i,s in enumerate(order,1):
  mp[i]=[tuple(w) for w in d['actions'][s]['words']];mp[-i]=[tuple(w) for w in iw[s]]
  aa[i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['auto']));aa[-i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['inverse']))
 def eva(w):
  f=ident
  for a in w:f=compose(f,aa[a])
  return f
 trans={ident:()};front=[ident]
 while front:
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   for a in (2,3,4,-4):
    h=compose(f,aa[a])
    if h not in trans:trans[h]=trans[f]+(a,);new.append(h)
  front=new
 assert len(trans)==48
 def normalize(w):
  out=();chunk=()
  for a in tuple(w)+(1,):
   if abs(a)==1:
    out=red(out+trans[eva(chunk)]+(a,));chunk=()
   else:chunk+=(a,)
  return out[:-1]
 def apply(a,w):
  out=red(y for x in w for y in (mp[a][x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[a][-x-1])))
  if len(out)>limit:raise OverflowError(('wordcap',a,len(out)))
  return out
 def image(aw,h):
  w=(h+1,)
  for a in reversed(aw):w=apply(a,w)
  return w
 b3=[(2,2),(4,4,4),(2,4,2,4),(3,3),(3,2)*4]
 z=(4,2,-4);b3.append((3,)+z+(-3,)+inv(z))
 rel=[];lengths=[]
 def addloop(aw):
  assert eva(aw)==ident
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(-h-1,)))
 for j,w in enumerate(b3):
  addloop(w);print('B3block',j,'maxlength',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for j,w in enumerate(ar['presentation_loops_left_composition']):
  nw=normalize(w);assert eva(nw)==ident
  addloop(nw)
  print('Autblock',j,'actionlength',len(nw),'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for g,w in enumerate(wd['words']):
  aw=normalize(w);assert eva(aw)==tuple(map(tuple,d['generators'][g]))
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(g+1,-h-1,-g-1)))
  print('Peifferblock',g,'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 out={'n':3,'endomorphisms':[mp[i] for i in (1,2,3,4,-1,-2,-3,-4)],'iterated_relators':rel,'fixed_relators':[],'proof':'B3 short presentation plus normalized complete Aut relators plus Peiffer; exact inverse endomorphisms included'}
 Path('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json').write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('Saved',len(rel),'relators totalletters',sum(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':generate()
''')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_lpres_cover.py').write_text('''"""Exact integral nilpotent quotients of the certified central IA3 cover.
This is NOT a presentation of IA3; central source artifacts may remain.
"""
import json,time,sys
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap

def build():
 assert bool(libgap.eval('LoadPackage("lpres",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)'))
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'))
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(9);gen=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  q=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:q=q*gen[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return q
 endos=[libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,F,gen,[word(w) for w in a]) for a in d['endomorphisms']]
 rels=[word(w) for w in d['iterated_relators'] if w]
 H=libgap.LPresentedGroup(F,[],endos,rels)
 return H

def run(c=3):
 H=build();t=time.time();Q=libgap.NilpotentQuotient(H,c)
 print('class',c,'integralNQtime',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 lcs=list(libgap.LowerCentralSeries(Q));invariants=[]
 for i in range(len(lcs)-1):
  inv=[int(x) for x in libgap.AbelianInvariants(libgap.FactorGroup(lcs[i],lcs[i+1]))]
  invariants.append(inv);print('LCSfactor',i+1,'counts',dict(Counter(inv)),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_cover_class%d_invariants.json'%c).write_text(json.dumps(invariants))
 return H,Q,invariants
if __name__=='__main__':run(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_lpres_cover import run
run(3)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_proof.md').write_text(r'''# Certified compact central L-cover of IA_3

Input: `agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json`; generator: `agent2_compact_cover_seeds.py`.
There are nine free Magnus generators, eight free-group automorphisms
(forward/inverse T,P,I,C), and 261 iterated relators, only 107 nonempty,
total 4990 letters, longest 682. No fixed relators are needed.

## Exact action lifts
The four Aut(F3) generators are T:x0->x0*x1, P swapping x0,x1,
I inverting x0, C cyclically permuting x0,x1,x2. Their conjugation
images of all nine Magnus generators are supplied as exact words in
`agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json`. Those identities were checked as
literal reduced free-group automorphisms. All four word maps are
actually automorphisms of the FREE group on nine symbols. Their
inverses are `agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json`; both substitution
composites reduce to each original generator, without any relations.

## Full signed-permutation action
First impose that these six operator words act trivially:
 P^2, C^3, (PC)^2, I^2, (IP)^4, [I,CPC^-1].
They present B3=C2^3 semidirect S3: P,C present S3; CPC^-1 fixes
coordinate0, so the last relation makes I invariant under its
stabilizer. Its three S3-conjugates are thus well defined involutions;
(IP)^4 says two distinct conjugates commute, and S3 conjugation makes
all three commute. Hence the presented group has order at most48;
its actual signed-permutation images have order48, giving equality.
Each operator loop is imposed on every one of the nine generators.

Consequently any consecutive signed-permutation subword of a longer
operator word may be replaced by a shortest representative of its
actual signed permutation. The generator uses an exhaustively checked
48-state Cayley table for this shortening. This is a proved operator
identity in the quotient, NOT an assumed free-endomorphism identity.

## Aut(F3) action and Peiffer relations
The complete four-generator Aut(F3) presentation is encoded and checked
in `agent4_AutF3_four_generator_presentation.json` (Tietze expansion
of GAP/FGA's classical Nielsen S,T,U presentation). All fourteen of
its loops are shortened as above and imposed on all nine generators.
Together with the B3 action relations this makes the operator action
factor through Aut(F3).

For every Magnus generator g_i choose its exact Aut(F3) word W_i from
`agent2_magnus_aut_words_n3.json`. Signed-permutation shortening is
again valid. Impose all81 Peiffer relations
 phi_(W_i)(g_j) * g_i * g_j^-1 * g_i^-1 = 1.
The Magnus images of W_i were independently checked as exact free-group
automorphisms. Every one of the resulting261 seed words was additionally
checked by literal evaluation in IA_3, not merely truncated expansions;
all evaluate to identity, with maximum intermediate total F3 wordlength177.

Let H be the invariant L-presented group, where the entire orbit of
all these relations under the eight operators is imposed. Thus all
operators and inverses descend to automorphisms of H. The map sending
the nine generators to the actual Magnus generators gives a surjection
mu:H->IA_3 by Magnus generation and is equivariant for Aut(F3).
Peiffer on generators extends to every x,y in H:
 phi_(mu(x))(y)=x*y*x^-1.
If mu(x)=1 this forces x central. Therefore ker(mu) is central.
No assertion H=IA_3 is made.

## Integral nilpotent audit
`agent2_lpres_cover.py` loads LPRES with OnlyNeeded:=true and computes
its integral nilpotent quotients entirely inside GAP (no external nq).
Class3 finishes in0.3sec. The lower-central factors are
 Z^9, Z^18, Z^43 directsum (Z/2)^14 directsum (Z/3)^9.
This agrees exactly with the separately audited class3 BCH computation.
These extra torsion factors may be artifacts of the central kernel;
they prove NO IA_3 discrepancy by themselves.
''')
print('wrote compactcover proof')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_cover_certificate.py').write_text('''"""Standalone literal-word audit of compact central-cover data."""
import json
from degree2_certificate import red,inv,compose
from agent2_compact_cover_seeds import generate

def check():
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'))
 def sub(w,mp):return red(y for x in w for y in (mp[x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[-x-1])))
 for s in ['T','P','I','C']:
  fw=d['actions'][s]['words'];bw=iw[s]
  for i in range(9):assert sub(fw[i],bw)==(i+1,) and sub(bw[i],fw)==(i+1,)
 gs=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in d['generators']];gis=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in d['inverses']];ident=((1,),(2,),(3,))
 def ev(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 for s in ['T','P','I','C']:
  a=d['actions'][s];t=tuple(map(tuple,a['auto']));ti=tuple(map(tuple,a['inverse']))
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  for g,w in zip(gs,a['words']):assert ev(w)==compose(t,compose(g,ti))
 out=generate()
 for w in out['iterated_relators']:assert ev(w)==ident
 print('PASS literalfreeinverseactions, exactAutconjugations, all261seedidentities')
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':check()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_cover_certificate import check
check()
```

```
                        Add( roots, [ [ - i, id ], [ i, w ] ] );
                        iw := ReduceWordKB( w ^ -1, inv );
                        Add( roots, [ [ - i, id ], [ i, iw ] ] );
                        rpos[i] := true;
                    fi;
                fi;
            od;
        fi;
    od;
    SetRootPositions( G, rpos );
    return roots;
end

 ExpandLogSequence
function ( mG, J )
    local fmG, gfmG, invrels, ninvrels, invrules, ninvrules, gprels, len, id, w, i, m, u, r;
    fmG := FreeGroupOfPresentation( mG );
    gfmG := GeneratorsOfGroup( fmG );
    invrels := InverseRelatorsOfPresentation( mG );
    ninvrels := Length( invrels );
    invrules := InverseRulesOfPresentation( mG );
    ninvrules := Length( invrules );
    gprels := GroupRelatorsOfPresentation( mG );
    len := Length( J );
    id := One( fmG );
    w := id;
    for i in [ 1 .. len ] do
        m := J[i][1];
        u := J[i][2];
        if m > 0 then
            r := gprels[m];
        else
            r := gprels[- m] ^ -1;
        fi;
        w := w * u ^ -1 * r * u;
        w := ReduceWordKB( w, invrules );
    od;
    return w;
end
```

```
Path('workspace/agent2_idrel_gl3.py').write_text('''"""Partial logged Knuth-Bendix produces exact GL3 relator identities.
No convergence or completeness claim. Every extracted identity is checked
as a literal free-group word before being used.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
from degree2_certificate import red,inv

def run(passes=1):
 assert bool(libgap.eval('LoadPackage("idrel",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)'))
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'))
 raw=d['aut_operator_relators']+d['magnus_aut_words_normalized'];na=len(d['aut_operator_relators'])
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(4);gg=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  q=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:q=q*gg[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return q
 G=F/libgap([word(w) for w in raw]);libgap.set_global('AG2GL',G)
 libgap.eval('(function() ArrangeMonoidGenerators(AG2GL); AG2M:=MonoidPresentationFpGroup(AG2GL); return true; end)()')
 libgap.eval('AG2Ids:=[]')
 f=libgap.MethodsOperation(libgap.LoggedOnePassKB,2)[-1]['func']
 src=str(f).replace('redrule := LogSequenceReduce( mG, L );','Add( AG2Ids, L ); redrule := LogSequenceReduce( mG, L );')
 libgap.eval('AG2OnePass:='+src)
 libgap.eval('(function() AG2Rules:=InitialLoggedRulesOfPresentation(AG2M); return true; end)()')
 print('Initialrules',libgap.eval('Length(AG2Rules)'),flush=True)
 for j in range(passes):
  libgap.eval('(function() AG2Rules:=AG2OnePass(AG2M,AG2Rules); return true; end)()')
  print('pass',j+1,'rules',libgap.eval('Length(AG2Rules)'),'identities',libgap.eval('Length(AG2Ids)'),flush=True)
  libgap.eval('(function() AG2Rules:=LoggedRewriteReduce(AG2M,AG2Rules); return true; end)()')
  print('reducedrules',libgap.eval('Length(AG2Rules)'),flush=True)
 # Export via the monoid presentation homomorphism to the original F4.
 data=libgap.eval('List(AG2Ids,J->List(J,c->[c[1],LetterRepAssocWord(Image(HomomorphismOfPresentation(AG2M),c[2]))]))').sage()
 original=libgap.eval('List(RelatorsOfFpGroup(AG2GL),LetterRepAssocWord)').sage()
 mapped=libgap.eval('List(GroupRelatorsOfPresentation(AG2M),r->LetterRepAssocWord(Image(HomomorphismOfPresentation(AG2M),r)))').sage()
 assert [red(w) for w in original]==[red(w) for w in mapped]
 good=[]
 for J in data:
  p=()
  for ri,u in J:
   r=tuple(original[abs(ri)-1]);r=r if ri>0 else inv(r)
   p=red(p+inv(u)+r+tuple(u))
  assert not p,('badidentity',J,p)
  good.append([[int(ri),list(map(int,u))] for ri,u in J])
 out={'aut_relator_count':na,'presentation_relators':original,'identities':good}
 Path('workspace/agent2_idrel_gl3_identities.json').write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('PASS',len(good),'exactfreeAutrelatoridentities',flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')
```

content:
```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_idrel_translate_c3.py').write_text('''"""Translate checked GL3 relator identities into genuine IA3 cubic vectors."""
import json,pickle
from pathlib import Path
from functools import lru_cache
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import mul,add,expand
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector
from degree2_certificate import inv

def run():
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_idrel_gl3_identities.json'));na=data['aut_relator_count']
 ad=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'));iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'))
 mp={}
 for i,s in enumerate(['T','P','I','C'],1):
  mp[i]=[add(expand(w),{():1},-1) for w in ad['actions'][s]['words']]
  mp[-i]=[add(expand(w),{():1},-1) for w in iw[s]]
 @lru_cache(None)
 def subword(a,w):
  p={():1}
  for i in w:p=mul(p,mp[a][i])
  return p
 def apply(a,p):
  o={}
  for w,x in p.items():o=add(o,subword(a,w),x)
  return o
 out=[]
 for J in data['identities']:
  p={():1}
  for ri,u in J:
   if abs(ri)<=na:continue
   gi=abs(ri)-na;term=expand((gi if ri>0 else -gi,))
   # Term is u^-1 r u, hence apply conjugation by u^-1.
   for a in reversed(inv(u)):term=apply(a,term)
   p=mul(p,term)
  assert not any(len(w)==1 for w in p)
  out.append(initial_vector(p))
 Path('workspace/agent2_idrel_c3_vectors.json').write_text(json.dumps([[int(x) for x in v] for v in out]))
 H,C,B=pickle.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_degree3_cache.pkl').read_bytes())
 HH=H.stack(Matrix(ZZ,out)).hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False)
 print('translated',len(out),'trueIArelations; oldHNFunchanged?',HH==H,flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent2_idrel_translate_c3 import run
run()

```

```
function ( arg... )
    local R, idnum, Dimension, Boundary, Elts, Mult, Inv, Frels, rels, Fgens, gens, FirstBoundaryHomomorphism, Boundary2Relator, start, ActWord, idnt, gennum, CommonChord, Amalgamate, b, r, x, i, X;
    R := arg[1];
    idnum := arg[2];
    if not (IsHapResolution( R ) or IsHapNonFreeResolution( R )) then
        Print( "This function must be applied to a resolution. \n" );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if not EvaluateProperty( R, "reduced" ) = true then
        if R!.dimension( 0 ) > 1 then
            Print( "This function must be applied to a REDUCED resolution. \n" );
            return fail;
        fi;
    fi;
    if not EvaluateProperty( R, "characteristic" ) = 0 then
        Print( "This function only works in characteristic 0. \n" );
        return fail;
    fi;
    Dimension := R!.dimension;
    Boundary := R!.boundary;
    Elts := R!.elts;
    Frels := [  ];
    start := List( [ 1 .. Dimension( 2 ) ], function ( x )
            return List( Boundary( 2, x ), function ( y )
                    return y[2];
                end );
        end );
    start := SortedList( Intersection( start ) )[1];
    gens := [  ];
    Mult := function ( g, h )
          return Position( Elts, Elts[g] * Elts[h] );
      end;
    Inv := function ( g )
          return Position( Elts, Elts[g] ^ -1 );
      end;
    FirstBoundaryHomomorphism := function ( x )
          local r;
          r := Boundary( 1, x[1] );
          r := List( r, function ( y )
                  return Mult( x[2], y[2] );
              end );
          if x[1] > 0 then
              return r;
          else
              return Reversed( r );
          fi;
          return;
      end;
    Boundary2Relator := function ( b )
          local c, rel, w;
          b := SortedList( AlgebraicReduction( b ) );
          rel := [ start ];
          while Length( b ) > 0 do
              for x in b do
                  w := FirstBoundaryHomomorphism( x );
                  if w[1] = rel[Length( rel )] then
                      Append( rel, [ w[2] ] );
                      RemoveSet( b, x );
                      break;
                  else
                      if w[2] = rel[Length( rel )] then
                          Append( rel, [ w[1] ] );
                          RemoveSet( b, x );
                          break;
                      fi;
                  fi;
              od;
          od;
          return rel;
      end;
    for r in [ 1 .. Dimension( 2 ) ] do
        Append( Frels, [ Boundary2Relator( Boundary( 2, r ) ) ] );
    od;
    for r in Frels do
        if not Inv( r[2] ) in gens then
            AddSet( gens, r[2] );
        fi;
        if not Inv( r[Length( r ) - 1] ) in gens then
            AddSet( gens, r[Length( r ) - 1] );
        fi;
    od;
    gennum := function ( r )
          local g, h;
          for g in gens do
              if Mult( r[1], g ) = r[2] then
                  h := Position( gens, g );
                  break;
              fi;
              if Mult( r[1], Inv( g ) ) = r[2] then
                  h := Position( gens, g );
                  break;
              fi;
          od;
          h := (h - 1) mod 6;
          h := h + 1;
          return h;
      end;
    CommonChord := function ( A, BB )
          local B, C, i, chord, chordA, chordB, comp;
          B := StructuralCopy( BB );
          C := Intersection( A, B );
          if Length( C ) < 2 then
              return fail;
          fi;
          chordA := [  ];
          chordB := [  ];
          for i in [ 1 .. Length( B ) - 1 ] do
              if B[i] in C and B[i + 1] in C then
                  Add( chordB, [ B[i], B[i + 1] ] );
              fi;
          od;
          for i in [ 1 .. Length( A ) - 1 ] do
              if A[i] in C and A[i + 1] in C then
                  Add( chordA, [ A[i], A[i + 1] ] );
              fi;
          od;
          chord := Intersection( chordA, chordB );
          if not Length( chord ) = Length( C ) - 1 then
              return fail;
          fi;
          if not B[1] in C then
              chord := List( chordB, function ( x )
                      return x[1];
                  end );
              Add( chord, chordB[Length( chordB )][2] );
              chordA := Reversed( B{[ 1 .. Position( B, chord[1] ) ]} );
              chordB := Reversed( B{[ Position( B, chord[Length( chord )] ) .. Length( B ) - 1 ]} );
              comp := Concatenation( chordA, chordB );
          fi;
          if B[1] in C then
              chordA := [  ];
              chordB := [  ];
              for x in B{[ 2 .. Length( B ) ]} do
                  if x in C then
                      Add( chordA, x );
                  else
                      break;
                  fi;
              od;
              for x in Reversed( B ) do
                  if x in C then
                      Add( chordB, x );
                  else

                  fi;

              chord := Concatenation( Reversed( chordB ), chordA );
              if B[2] in C and B[Length( B ) - 1] in C and B[2] in C then
                  comp := Reversed( B{[ Position( B, chord[Length( chord )] ) .. Position( B, chord[1] ) ]} );
              fi;
              if B[2] in C and not B[Length( B ) - 1] in C then
                  comp := Reversed( B{[ Position( B, chord[Length( chord )] ) .. Length( B ) ]} );
              fi;
              if not B[2] in C and B[Length( B ) - 1] in C then
                  comp := Reversed( B{[ 1 .. Position( B, chord[1] ) ]} );
              fi;
          fi;
          return [ chord, comp ];
      end;
    Amalgamate := function ( A, B )
          local C, D, Begin, End, amalg;
          D := CommonChord( A, B );
          C := D[2];
          if C = fail then
              return fail;
          fi;
          if not A[1] in D[1] then
              Begin := A{[ 1 .. Position( A, C[1] ) - 1 ]};
              End := A{[ Position( A, C[Length( C )] ) + 1 .. Length( A ) ]};
              amalg := Concatenation( Begin, C, End );
          fi;
          if A[1] in D[1] then
              End := A{[ Position( A, C[Length( C )] ) + 1 .. Position( A, C[1] ) - 1 ]};
              amalg := Concatenation( C, End );
              Add( amalg, C[1] );
          fi;
          return amalg;
      end;
    idnt := [  ];
    for b in Boundary( 3, idnum ) do
        x := Boundary( 2, AbsInt( b[1] ) );
        x := Boundary2Relator( x );
        if SignInt( b[1] ) = -1 then

        fi;
        x := List( x, function ( t )
                return Mult( b[2], t );
            end );
        Add( idnt, x );
    od;
    return [ Amalgamate, idnt ];
end
```

```
"""Extract explicit free-group identities among SL3 relators from HAP3cells.
Every emitted identity is checked by literal free-word reduction.
"""
import json
from functools import lru_cache
from agent1_degree3 import red,inv

D=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_presentation_3cells_simple.json'))
E=[tuple(x for r in g for x in r) for g in D['matrix_elts']]
def mm(a,b):return tuple(sum(a[3*i+k]*b[3*k+j] for k in range(3)) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))
def endpoints(e,m):
 b=D['boundaries']['1'][e-1]
 n=next(E[i-1] for s,i in b if s<0);p=next(E[i-1] for s,i in b if s>0)
 return mm(m,n),mm(m,p)

def baseface(f):
 edges=[]
 for e,i in D['boundaries']['2'][f]:
  m=E[i-1];u,v=endpoints(abs(e),m)
  if e<0:u,v=v,u
  edges.append((u,v,e,(abs(e),m),1 if e>0 else -1))
 r=D['relators'][f]
 def dfs(out,unused):
  if not unused:return out if out[-1][1]==out[0][0] else None
  label=r[len(out)]
  for j in unused:
   ee=edges[j]
   if ee[0]==out[-1][1] and ee[2]==label:
    z=dfs(out+[ee],unused-{j})
    if z:return z
  return None
 for j,ee in enumerate(edges):
  if ee[2]==r[0]:
   z=dfs([ee],set(range(len(edges)))-{j})
   if z:return z
 raise ValueError(('no attaching polygon',f,r))

def cancel(poly):
 out=[]
 for ee in poly:
  if out and out[-1][3]==ee[3] and out[-1][4]==-ee[4]:out.pop()
  else:out.append(ee)
 # Do not cyclicallycancel acrossbasepoint; linearcancellationenough.
 return out

def labels(poly):return tuple(e[2] for e in poly)
def translate(poly,g,sgn):
 out=[]
 for u,v,e,(idx,m),s in poly:out.append((mm(g,u),mm(g,v),e,(idx,mm(g,m)),s))
 if sgn<0:out=[(v,u,-e,ID,-s) for u,v,e,ID,s in out[::-1]]
 return out

def termsword(terms):
 out=()
 for q,f,s in terms:
  r=tuple(D['relators'][f]);r=r if s>0 else inv(r)
  out=red(out+q+r+inv(q))
 return out

def main():
 faces=[baseface(f) for f in range(len(D['relators']))]
 allident=[]
 for cell,b3 in enumerate(D['boundaries']['3']):
  pol=[]
  for f,i in b3:
   sign=1 if f>0 else -1
   pol.append((translate(faces[abs(f)-1],E[i-1],sign),[((),abs(f)-1,sign)]))
  while len(pol)>1:
   found=None
   for ia in range(len(pol)):
    for ib in range(ia+1,len(pol)):
     A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib]
     for a,ea in enumerate(A):
      for b,eb in enumerate(B):
       if ea[3]==eb[3] and ea[4]==-eb[4]:found=(ia,ib,a,b);break
      if found:break
     if found:break
    if found:break
   if found is None:break
   ia,ib,a,b=found;A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib]
   S=labels(A[:a+1]);Q=labels(B[:b]);conj=red(S+inv(Q))
   Brot=B[b:]+B[:b];new=cancel(A[:a]+Brot[1:]+A[a+1:])
   assert red(conj+labels(B)+inv(conj)+labels(A))==red(labels(new))
   terms=[(red(conj+q),f,s) for q,f,s in tb]+ta
   assert termsword(terms)==red(labels(new))
   for i in sorted((ia,ib),reverse=True):pol.pop(i)
   pol.append((new,terms))
  if len(pol)==1 and not red(labels(pol[0][0])):
   terms=pol[0][1];assert not termsword(terms)
   allident.append({'cell':cell+1,'terms':terms})
   print('VERIFIED FREE IDENTITY cell',cell+1,'terms',len(terms),flush=True)
  else:print('cell',cell+1,'not reduced toidentity; remainingpolygons',len(pol),'lengths',[len(p) for p,t in pol],flush=True)
 json.dump(allident,open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_free_identities.json','w'))
 return allident
if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

```
faces=[baseface(f) for f in range(len(D['relators']))]
def cycred(p,t=None):
 k=0
 while 2*k<len(p) and p[k][3]==p[-k-1][3] and p[k][4]==-p[-k-1][4]:k+=1
 if not k:return (p,t) if t is not None else p
 v=inv(labels(p[:k]));p=p[k:len(p)-k]
 if t is None:return p
 return p,[(red(v+q),f,s) for q,f,s in t]

def solve(cell,seed=0):
 rng=random.Random(seed);pol=[]
 for f,i in D['boundaries']['3'][cell-1]:
  s=1 if f>0 else -1
  p,t=cycred(translate(faces[abs(f)-1],E[i-1],s),[((),abs(f)-1,s)])
  pol.append((p,t))
 rng.shuffle(pol)
 while len(pol)>1:
  lookup=defaultdict(list)
  for ia,(A,ta) in enumerate(pol):
   for a,ea in enumerate(A):lookup[ea[3],ea[4]].append((ia,a))
  best=None;score=-1;ties=0
  for (ID,sgn),occs in lookup.items():
   if sgn<0:continue
   for ia,a in occs:
    for ib,b in lookup.get((ID,-sgn),[]):
     if ia==ib:continue
     A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib];Br=B[b:]+B[:b]
     nn=cycred(cancel(A[:a]+Br[1:]+A[a+1:]))
     sc=len(A)+len(B)-len(nn)
     if sc>score:best=(ia,ib,a,b);score=sc;ties=1
     elif sc==score:
      ties+=1
      if rng.randrange(ties)==0:best=(ia,ib,a,b)
  if best is None:return None,[len(p) for p,t in pol]
  ia,ib,a,b=best;A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib]
  conj=red(labels(A[:a+1])+inv(labels(B[:b])))
  Br=B[b:]+B[:b];new=cancel(A[:a]+Br[1:]+A[a+1:])
  terms=[(red(conj+q),f,s) for q,f,s in tb]+ta
  new,terms=cycred(new,terms)
  for z in sorted((ia,ib),reverse=True):pol.pop(z)
  pol.append((new,terms))
 if not red(labels(pol[0][0])):
  assert not termsword(pol[0][1]);return pol[0][1],[]
 return None,[len(pol[0][0])]

def run(cells=range(8,13),tries=30):
 out=[]
 for c in cells:
  for seed in range(tries):
   ans,lens=solve(c,seed)
   if ans is not None:
    print('VERIFIED cell',c,'seed',seed,'terms',len(ans),flush=True);out.append({'cell':c,'terms':ans});break
   print('cell',c,'try',seed,'remaining',lens,flush=True)
  json.dump(out,open('workspace/agent2_HAP_extra_free_identities.json','w'))
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_fast_bch3.py').write_text('''"""Integral class3 BCH coordinates (log1,2log2,12log3), exact Aut actions."""
import json,pickle
from pathlib import Path
from sage.all import Matrix,vector,ZZ,identity_matrix,zero_vector
from agent1_degree3 import mul,add,br,expand,coords,LP,L3,m
from agent1_degree3_closure import action_matrix,coords2,L2,pairs

n2=len(pairs);n3=len(L3);dim=m+n2+n3

def wordlog(w):
 ex=expand(w);f=[{u:x for u,x in ex.items() if len(u)==i} for i in range(1,4)];a,b,c=f
 diag={(i,i):x for (i,),x in a.items()};cube={(i,i,i):x for (i,),x in a.items()}
 q=add(add({u:2*x for u,x in b.items()},diag),mul(a,a),-1)
 cross={}
 for (i,j),x in b.items():cross=add(cross,{(i,i,j):x,(i,j,j):x}) if i!=j else add(cross,{(i,i,i):2*x})
 t={u:12*x for u,x in c.items()};t=add(t,cross,6);t=add(t,cube,2)
 t=add(t,add(mul(a,b),mul(b,a)),-6);t=add(t,add(mul(a,diag),mul(diag,a)),-3);t=add(t,mul(mul(a,a),a),4)
 return vector(ZZ,[a.get((i,),0) for i in range(m)]+coords2(q)+coords(t))

BR13=[]
for i in range(m):
 cols=[coords(br({(i,):1},p)) for p in L2]
 BR13.append(Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose())

def br11(a,d):return vector(ZZ,[a[i]*d[j]-a[j]*d[i] for i,j in pairs])
def br13(a,b):
 ans=zero_vector(ZZ,n3);bb=vector(ZZ,b)
 for i,x in enumerate(a):
  if x:ans+=x*(BR13[i]*bb)
 return ans

def bch(x,y):
 a,b,c=x[:m],x[m:m+n2],x[m+n2:];d,e,f=y[:m],y[m:m+n2],y[m+n2:]
 ad=br11(a,d)
 v3=vector(ZZ,c)+vector(ZZ,f)+3*(br13(a,e)-br13(d,b))+br13(a,ad)-br13(d,ad)
 return vector(ZZ,list(vector(ZZ,a)+vector(ZZ,d))+list(vector(ZZ,b)+vector(ZZ,e)+ad)+list(v3))

def actions():
 cp=Path('workspace/agent2_fast_bch3_actions.pkl')
 if cp.exists():return pickle.loads(cp.read_bytes())
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'));iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'));out={}
 for i,s in enumerate(['T','P','I','C'],1):
  for sign,wds in [(1,d['actions'][s]['words']),(-1,iw[s])]:
   old=action_matrix(wds);cols=[list(wordlog(w)) for w in wds]
   for j in range(n2):cols.append([0]*m+list(old[:n2,j])+list(3*old[n2:,j]))
   for j in range(n3):cols.append([0]*(m+n2)+list(old[n2:,n2+j]))
   A=Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose();out[sign*i]=A
  assert out[i]*out[-i]==identity_matrix(ZZ,dim)
 cp.write_bytes(pickle.dumps(out));return out

def actionword(w,acts=None):
 acts=actions() if acts is None else acts;A=identity_matrix(ZZ,dim)
 for i in w:A=A*acts[i]
 return A

def oldcoords(x):

 return [v//2 for v in b]+[v//6 for v in c]
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_hap_comparison_fillings.py').write_text('''"""Exact logged fillings of Aut two-cells in the HAP GL3 presentation.
Accept only literal free-word equality of each exported filling.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
from degree2_certificate import red,inv

def run(passes=3):
 libgap.eval('LoadPackage("idrel",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
 hd=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_GL3_product_identities.json'));R=hd['relators']
 cp=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'));ar=cp['aut_operator_relators']
 ic=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_HAP_inverse_comparison.json'))
 q={1:tuple(ic['T']),2:(4,)+tuple(ic['P']),3:(4,)+tuple(ic['I']),4:tuple(ic['C'])}
 def subst(w):return red(y for x in w for y in (q[x] if x>0 else inv(q[-x])))
 queries=[subst(w) for w in ar]
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(4);gen=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  g=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:g=g*gen[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return g
 G=F/libgap([word(w) for w in R]);libgap.set_global('AG2HG',G)
 libgap.eval('(function() ArrangeMonoidGenerators(AG2HG); AG2HM:=MonoidPresentationFpGroup(AG2HG); AG2HRules:=InitialLoggedRulesOfPresentation(AG2HM); return true; end)()')
 arr=[int(x) for x in libgap.ArrangeMonoidGenerators(G)];mg=list(libgap.eval('GeneratorsOfGroup(FreeGroupOfPresentation(AG2HM))'))
 def monword(w):

  for x in w:g=g*mg[arr.index(x)]

 mq=[monword(w) for w in queries];out={}

  for i,w in enumerate(mq):

   logs=result[0];terms=[];p=()

    r=tuple(R[abs(ri)-1]);r=r if ri>0 else inv(r)
    p=red(p+inv(u)+r+u);terms.append([ri,list(u)])
   if p!=queries[i]:

 return out
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent2_H4_full_audit.py').write_text('''"""Sanity audit of the purported central cover's class4 quotient.
No actual IA counterexample claim; all output hypotheses are tested.
"""
import json,time
from pathlib import Path
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
from agent1_degree3 import commuting_relators
from degree2_certificate import red,inv

def run():
 libgap.eval('LoadPackage("lpres",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'))
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(9);gen=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  q=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:q=q*gen[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return q
 endos=[libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,F,gen,[word(w) for w in a]) for a in d['endomorphisms'][:4]]
 rels=[word(w) for w in d['iterated_relators'] if w]+[word(w) for w,ex in commuting_relators()]
 H=libgap.LPresentedGroup(F,[],endos,rels)
 t=time.time();Q=libgap.NilpotentQuotient(H,4);e=libgap.NilpotentQuotientSystem(H)['Epimorphism'];R=libgap.Range(e)
 ims=[libgap.Image(e,g) for g in libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(H)]
 print('Q4computed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 # Export an exact pc presentation, allowing fast reconstruction in a new run.
 iso=libgap.IsomorphismFpGroup(R);fp=libgap.Range(iso)
 save={'generators':len(list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(fp))),'relators':[[int(x) for x in libgap.LetterRepAssocWord(r)] for r in libgap.RelatorsOfFpGroup(fp)],'original_images':[[int(x) for x in libgap.LetterRepAssocWord(libgap.Image(iso,g))] for g in ims]}
 Path('workspace/agent2_H4_pc_presentation.json').write_text(json.dumps(save))
 print('ExportedQ4presentation',save['generators'],'generators',len(save['relators']),'relations',flush=True)
 def ev(w):
  p=ims[0]**0
  for x in w:p=p*ims[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return p
 result={'seed_failures':[],'one_step_image_failures':[],'operator_homomorphisms':[]}
 def dump():Path('workspace/agent2_H4_full_audit.json').write_text(json.dumps(result))
 for i,w in enumerate(d['iterated_relators']):
  if not bool(libgap.IsOne(ev(w))):result['seed_failures'].append(i)
  for a,mp in enumerate(d['endomorphisms']):
   sw=red(y for x in w for y in (mp[x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[-x-1])))
   if not bool(libgap.IsOne(ev(sw))):result['one_step_image_failures'].append([i,a])
 print('Seedfailures',result['seed_failures'],'imagefailures',result['one_step_image_failures'],flush=True);dump()
 def comm(x,y):return x*y*x**-1*y**-1
 beta=comm(comm(ims[2],ims[8]),ims[7]);result['beta_order']=str(libgap.Order(beta));result['beta_brackets_isone']=[bool(libgap.IsOne(comm(beta,g))) for g in ims]
 print('beta',result['beta_order'],result['beta_brackets_isone'],flush=True);dump()
 for i,mp in enumerate(d['endomorphisms']):
  hom=libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(R,R,ims,[ev(w) for w in mp]);valid=bool(hom!=libgap.fail)
  result['operator_homomorphisms'].append(valid);print('operator',i,'descends?',valid,flush=True);dump()
 return result
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')

run()
```

For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer is \mathcal D=\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}: n\ge3,\ c\ge6\}. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it takes two halves: for every \(n\ge3\) and \(c\ge6\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\), and for every \(n\ge2\) and \(c\ge1\) with \(n=2\) or \(c\le5\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)=\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\). Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

### Mathematical logic of alarm
For central group kernel K of H→IA:
if x∈γcH maps intoγc+1IA, choose y∈γc+1H with same image; x y^-1∈Kcentral, so [x,H]⊂γc+2H. Thus graded kernel central.
β has J3=0. Desired c3 injectivity would imply β mapsγ4IA; hence all [β,gi]=0 in H4. They don’t.
Either central-cover construction/action complete presentation, LPRES quotient invariance, or user assertion is wrong. We are not claiming which.

### A few exact APIs / pitfalls
- Initialize Sage before libgap: `from sage.all import *` then `from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap`, otherwise ImportError.
- `libgap.eval` accepts **one statement only**, no trailing `;;`. Wrap multi statements inside `(function() ...; return true; end)()`.
- GAP lists in libgap **do not accept negative Python indices**; use `[len(x)-1]`.
- Sage Matrix `old[:n2,j]` is a matrix; `.list()` flattens, `list(...)` gives rowvectors.
- GAP raw pcp orders not Abelian invariants.
- Never compare pc group identities from unverified different families; `IsOne(x)` safest.
- IsomorphismFpGroup images are FpElements; use UnderlyingElement before LetterRepAssocWord.
- OnlyNeeded package loading essential for lpres/hap/idrel/FR to avoid hanging optional programs.
- External `nq` not usable; internal LPRES NQ is.
- Keep checkpoints before long operations; fresh Python loses all GAP state. Export pc presentation is now the way around repeated H4 recomputation.
